# Fair Market Rents: New Hampshire

- Postal code: NH
- State FIPS: 33
- Counties: 0
- Towns: 259

HUD's Fair Market Rents for New Hampshire, fiscal years 1983 to 2027: 259 towns, each with its rents by bedroom count and the FMR areas it has sat in. 4 of them have changed FMR area at least once (not counting 2006, when HUD renumbered every area).
